  docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:148:## Alternatives considered
  docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:149:
> docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:150:- **A confirmation dialog before each reveal** — 
rejected as friction theatre. An operator who
> docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:151:  reveals contact numbers many times a day gets no 
additional safety from clicking "confirm";
> docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:152:  the audit log is the real control, and it already 
records every reveal without requiring the
  docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:153:  admin to do anything extra.
> docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:154:- **Storing the raw number in the audit payload** — 
rejected: `audit_log` is readable by any role
> docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:155:  with `audit.read` (which is broader than the two roles 
allowed to reveal), so this would
  docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:156:  re-leak the exact PII the masking work exists to 
contain, just through a different door.
> docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:157:- **Reveal-by-CSV (a separate "unmasked export" admin 
action)** — rejected as unattributable at
  docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:158:  the row level: a CSV, once downloaded, carries no 
record of who looked at which specific
> docs\adr\0034-pii-masking-default-and-audited-reveal.md:159:  number, defeating the audit requirement entirely.

  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:50:    `{ party?, subjectRef?, reason }`. `subjectRef` is included only when

  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:51:    resolved before the denial; `party` is included whenever known (not 
yet known for `FORBIDDEN`,
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:52:    which is checked before the body is parsed). Never a raw id or a raw 
phone. Denials are written
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:53:    via `auditLog()` (best-effort, swallows its own failure to Sentry) 
rather than
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:54:    `appendAuditEntry()` (fails closed, used by the success path) — a 
lost denial-audit write must
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:55:    never turn an otherwise-correct 403/404/429 into a 500.
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:56:6b. The `FORBIDDEN` check runs inside `revealContactHandler` itself, as 
the first thing the handler
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:57:    does, rather than inside `requireAdmin` middleware — `requireAdmin` 
now authenticates any admin
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:58:    role for this route (`ALL_ADMIN_ROLES`) so an 
`AdminContext`/`adminId` exists to attribute the
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:60:    (`{ code: 'FORBIDDEN', requiredRoles: ['super-admin', 'ops-manager'] 
}`), built from the same
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:61:    exported `PII_REVEAL_ALLOWED_ROLES` const the authorization check 
itself uses so the two cannot
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:62:    drift. The pre-existing composed-handler RBAC test continues to pass 
unchanged against the
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:63:    actually-registered handler.
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:67:   anything ambiguous or absent resolves to `404 PARTY_NOT_AVAILABLE` 
rather than guessing.
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:68:8. `ContactReveal` (admin-web) shows the masked number at rest, a 
labelled "Show number" control
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:69:   (not an icon-only eye), reveals the full number for 60 seconds with a 
live "Hide now · Ns"
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:70:   countdown/manual-hide control, states inline that the reveal was 
recorded in the audit log,
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:71:   and renders 403/429/other-failure states inline (not as a toast) — 
with no confirmation dialog,
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:72:   since the audit log is the accountability mechanism.
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:73:9. `orders.revealContact` is a capability granted to `super-admin` and 
`ops-manager` only; the
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:74:   reveal control does not render for a role that lacks it.
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:75:10. `admin-web/messages/en.json` and `admin-web/messages/hi.json` hold 
471 keys each in exact key
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:76:    parity, with the PII-reveal copy translated into Devanagari rather 
than copied across from
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:77:    English, pinned against the real message files (not a mock) in
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:78:    `admin-web/tests/i18n/pii-copy.test.ts`.
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:81:
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:82:`POST /v1/admin/orders/{id}/reveal-contact`
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:83:
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:84:**Request body:**

> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:96:{ "party": "CUSTOMER", "phone": "+919876543210", "revealedAt": 
"2026-09-08T10:15:00.000Z" }
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:97:```
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:98:
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:104:|---|---|---|
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:105:| 200 | — | Full number returned; reveal audit-logged. |
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:106:| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` / `TOKEN_INVALID` / `SESSION_EXPIRED` | No 
valid admin session (`requireAdmin`). |
  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:107:| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Authenticated, but role is not `super-admin` or

  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:116:
> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:117:Every outcome above except `200`, `401`, and `422` also writes a

> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:118:entry (see Acceptance 6a/6b and `docs/runbook.md` → "Contact reveal

> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:119:audited: `requireAdmin` rejects the request before an

> docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:120:the row to. `422` is not audited: a malformed body carries no useful

  docs\stories\E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:121:what request logs already capture.

 succeeded in 560ms:
export type AdminRole = 'super-admin' | 'ops-manager' | 'finance' | 'support-agent';
'use client';

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { apiUrl } from '@/api/base';
import type { AdminRole } from '@/lib/auth/types';
import { getFirebaseAuth } from '@/lib/auth/firebase';
import { unregisterAdminPushToken } from '@/lib/push-registration';

export interface AuthState {
  adminId: string;
  email: string;
  role: AdminRole;
}

interface AdminAuthContextValue {
  auth: AuthState | null;
  setAuth: (auth: AuthState) => void;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AdminAuthContext = createContext<AdminAuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AdminAuthProvider({
  children,
  initialAuth,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  initialAuth: AuthState | null;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [auth, setAuth] = useState<AuthState | null>(initialAuth);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    // Best-effort: de-register FCM push token before invalidating the session.
    // Failures are swallowed so logout is never blocked.
    try {
      const currentUser = getFirebaseAuth().currentUser;
      if (currentUser) {
        const idToken = await currentUser.getIdToken();
        await unregisterAdminPushToken(idToken);
      }
    } catch {
      // Intentionally ignored â€” push de-registration is best-effort.
    }
    await fetch(apiUrl('/v1/admin/auth/logout'), { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' });
    setAuth(null);
    // Unprefixed '/login' is correct despite localePrefix: 'always'. The middleware resolves the
    // locale from the NEXT_LOCALE cookie when a path carries no prefix
    // (src/lib/i18n/helpers.ts:getLocaleFromRequest), and LocaleSwitcher sets that cookie â€” so a
    // Hindi admin lands on /hi/login. Prefixing it here by hand was tried and reverted: it adds an
    // i18n dependency to the auth provider for no behavioural gain.
    router.push('/login');
  }, [router]);

  return (
    <AdminAuthContext.Provider value={{ auth, setAuth, logout }}>
      {children}
    </AdminAuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAdminAuth(): AdminAuthContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AdminAuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAdminAuth must be used inside AdminAuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}

 221: export async function getTechniciansWithinRadius(
 222:   lat: number,
 223:   lng: number,
 224:   radiusKm: number,
 225:   serviceId: string,
 226: ): Promise<TechnicianProfile[]> {
 227:   const client = getCosmosClient();
 228:   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 229:   const polygon = boundingBoxPolygon(lat, lng, radiusKm);
 230:   const query = {
 231:     query: `SELECT * FROM c
 232:             WHERE ST_WITHIN(c.location, @polygon)
 233:             AND ARRAY_CONTAINS(c.skills, @serviceId)
 234:             AND c.isOnline = true
 235:             AND c.isAvailable = true`,
 236:     parameters: [
 237:       { name: '@polygon', value: polygon as unknown as string },
 238:       { name: '@serviceId', value: serviceId },
 239:     ],
 240:   };
 241:   const { resources } = await container.items
 242:     .query<TechnicianProfile>(query)
 243:     .fetchAll();
 244:   return resources;
 245: }

 247: export interface TechnicianLookupInfo {
 248:   id: string;
 249:   technicianId: string;
 250:   displayName?: string;
 251:   name?: string;
 252:   rating?: number;
 253:   isOnline?: boolean;
 254:   isAvailable?: boolean;
 255: }
 256: 
 257: export async function getTechniciansByIds(
 258:   technicianIds: string[],
 259: ): Promise<TechnicianLookupInfo[]> {
 260:   const ids = [...new Set(technicianIds.filter(Boolean))];
 261:   if (ids.length === 0) return [];
 262: 
 263:   const client = getCosmosClient();
 264:   const container = client.database(DB_NAME).container(CONTAINER);
 265:   const { resources } = await container.items
 266:     .query<TechnicianLookupInfo>({
 267:       query: `SELECT c.id, c.technicianId, c.displayName, c.name, c.rating, c.isOnline, c.isAvailable
 268:               FROM c
 269:               WHERE ARRAY_CONTAINS(@ids, c.id) OR ARRAY_CONTAINS(@ids, c.technicianId)`,
 270:       parameters: [{ name: '@ids', value: ids }],
 271:     })
 272:     .fetchAll();
 273:   return resources;
 274: }

 276: function technicianDisplayName(tech: TechnicianProfile & Record<string, unknown>): string {
 277:   const displayName = tech['displayName'];
 278:   if (typeof displayName === 'string' && displayName.trim() !== '') return displayName;
 279:   const name = tech['name'];
 280:   if (typeof name === 'string' && name.trim() !== '') return name;
 281:   return tech.technicianId || tech.id;
 282: }

import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { OrdersTable } from '../src/components/orders/OrdersTable';
import type { Order } from '../src/types/order';

vi.mock('next-intl', () => ({
  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => {
    const map: Record<string, string> = {
      'table.columns.orderId': 'Order ID',
      'table.columns.customer': 'Customer',
      'table.columns.service': 'Service',
      'table.columns.technician': 'Technician',
      'table.columns.status': 'Status',
      'table.columns.city': 'City',
      'table.columns.scheduled': 'Scheduled',
      'table.columns.amount': 'Amount',
      'table.columns.action': 'Action',
      'table.emptyState': 'No orders found',
      'table.viewAction': 'View â†’',
      'table.pagination.prev': 'Prev',
      'table.pagination.next': 'Next',
      // StatusBadge statuses â€” ASSIGNED not in catalog, fallback used
      'statuses.COMPLETED': 'Completed',
    };
    return map[key] ?? key;
  },
  useLocale: () => 'en',
}));

const sampleOrder: Order = {
  id: 'ord_12345678', customerId: 'cust_1', customerName: 'Rahul Sharma',
  customerPhone: '9999999999', status: 'ASSIGNED', city: 'Bengaluru',
  scheduledAt: new Date().toISOString(), amount: 599, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
};

describe('OrdersTable', () => {
  const baseProps = {
    orders: [sampleOrder], total: 1, page: 1, pageSize: 50,
    totalPages: 1, isLoading: false,
    onRowClick: vi.fn(), onPageChange: vi.fn(), canReveal: false,
  };

  it('renders customer name in a row', () => {
    render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Rahul Sharma')).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('renders StatusBadge with status text (fallback for unknown status)', () => {
    render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} />);
    // ASSIGNED not in catalog â†’ falls back to raw status string
    expect(screen.getByText('ASSIGNED')).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('calls onRowClick with order when row is clicked', () => {
    render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Rahul Sharma'));
    expect(baseProps.onRowClick).toHaveBeenCalledWith(sampleOrder);
  });

  it('Prev button is disabled on page 1', () => {
    render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} />);
    expect((screen.getByLabelText('Previous page') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it('Next button is disabled when page equals totalPages', () => {
    render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} page={1} totalPages={1} />);
    expect((screen.getByLabelText('Next page') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it('renders a reveal control for the customer phone when permitted', () => {
    render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} canReveal={true} />);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /show/i }).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('renders no reveal control when the role lacks the capability', () => {
    render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} canReveal={false} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /show/i })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('uses token classes (not raw gray/white) for table chrome', () => {
    const { container } = render(<OrdersTable {...baseProps} />);
    // The outer wrapper must use token border, not Tailwind gray
    const wrapper = container.querySelector('div.overflow-x-auto');
    expect(wrapper?.className).toMatch(/border-\[var\(--color-border\)\]/);
    expect(wrapper?.className).not.toMatch(/border-gray-200/);

    // Header row uses surface-alt
    const thead = container.querySelector('thead');
    expect(thead?.className).toMatch(/bg-\[var\(--color-surface-alt\)\]/);
    expect(thead?.className).not.toMatch(/bg-gray-50/);

    // Body uses surface (canvas) â€” never bg-white
    const tbody = container.querySelector('tbody');
    expect(tbody?.className).not.toMatch(/bg-white/);
  });
});

import { app } from '@azure/functions';
import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponseInit, InvocationContext } from '@azure/functions';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { requireAdmin } from '../../../middleware/requireAdmin.js';
import type { AdminContext } from '../../../types/admin.js';
import { getOrderById } from '../../../cosmos/orders-repository.js';
import { updateBookingFields, bookingRepo } from '../../../cosmos/booking-repository.js';
import { appendAuditEntry } from '../../../cosmos/audit-log-repository.js';
import {
  ReassignBodySchema,
  CompleteBodySchema,
  RefundBodySchema,
  WaiveFeeBodySchema,
  EscalateBodySchema,
  NoteBodySchema,
} from '../../../schemas/order-overrides.js';

function getOrderId(req: HttpRequest): string | undefined {
  const params = req.params as Record<string, string | undefined>;
  return params['id'];
}

// â”€â”€ reassign â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

export async function reassignOrderHandler(
  req: HttpRequest,
  _ctx: InvocationContext,
  admin: AdminContext,
): Promise<HttpResponseInit> {
  const id = getOrderId(req);
  if (!id) return { status: 400, jsonBody: { code: 'MISSING_ID' } };

  const raw = await req.json();
  const parsed = ReassignBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return { status: 422, jsonBody: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', details: parsed.error.flatten() } };
  }

  const updated = await updateBookingFields(id, { technicianId: parsed.data.technicianId });
  if (!updated) {
    return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'ORDER_NOT_FOUND' } };
  }

  const order = await getOrderById(id);
  await appendAuditEntry({
    id: randomUUID(),
    adminId: admin.adminId,
    role: admin.role,
    action: 'REASSIGN',
    resourceType: 'booking',
    resourceId: id,
    payload: { technicianId: parsed.data.technicianId, reason: parsed.data.reason },
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    partitionKey: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7),
  });

  return { status: 200, jsonBody: order };
}

});

  }

  const updated = await updateBookingFields(id, { status: 'COMPLETED' });
  if (!updated) {
    return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'ORDER_NOT_FOUND' } };
  }

  const order = await getOrderById(id);

    payload: { reason: parsed.data.reason },
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    partitionKey: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7),
  });

  return { status: 200, jsonBody: order };
}

export async function noteOrderHandler(
  req: HttpRequest,
  _ctx: InvocationContext,
  admin: AdminContext,
): Promise<HttpResponseInit> {
  const id = getOrderId(req);
  if (!id) return { status: 400, jsonBody: { code: 'MISSING_ID' } };

  const raw = await req.json();
  const parsed = NoteBodySchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return { status: 422, jsonBody: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', details: parsed.error.flatten() } };
  }

  const existingBooking = await bookingRepo.getById(id);
  if (!existingBooking) {
    return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'ORDER_NOT_FOUND' } };
  }

  const updatedNotes = [...(existingBooking.internalNotes ?? []), parsed.data.note];
  await updateBookingFields(id, { internalNotes: updatedNotes });

  const order = await getOrderById(id);
  await appendAuditEntry({
    id: randomUUID(),
    adminId: admin.adminId,
    role: admin.role,
    action: 'ADD_NOTE',
    resourceType: 'booking',
    resourceId: id,
    payload: { note: parsed.data.note },
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    partitionKey: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7),
  });

  return { status: 200, jsonBody: order };
}

  methods: ['POST'],
  route: 'v1/admin/orders/{id}/note',
  authLevel: 'anonymous',
  handler: requireAdmin(['super-admin', 'ops-manager'])(noteOrderHandler),
});

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getApiBaseUrl } from '@/lib/apiBase';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

const LEGACY_REFRESH_PROXY_PATH = '/admin-api/v1/admin/auth/refresh';
const SETUP_TOTP_PATH = 'v1/admin/auth/setup-totp';

type ProxyContext = {
  params: Promise<{ path?: string[] }>;
};

function splitSetCookieHeader(value: string): string[] {
  return value
    .split(/,(?=\s*[^;,=]+=[^;,]*)/g)
    .map((cookie) => cookie.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function getSetCookieHeaders(headers: Headers): string[] {
  const withGetter = headers as Headers & { getSetCookie?: () => string[] };
  const setCookies = withGetter.getSetCookie?.();
  if (setCookies && setCookies.length > 0) return setCookies;

  const combined = headers.get('set-cookie');
  return combined ? splitSetCookieHeader(combined) : [];
}

function isLocalhost(requestUrl: string): boolean {
  const host = new URL(requestUrl).hostname;
  return host === 'localhost' || host === '127.0.0.1' || host === '::1';
}

function rewriteSetCookie(cookie: string, requestUrl: string): string {
  let rewritten = cookie.replace(
    /;\s*Path=\/api\/v1\/admin\/auth\/refresh/gi,
    '; Path=/',
  );

  if (/^hs_setup=/i.test(cookie.trim())) {
    rewritten = rewritten.replace(/;\s*Path=\/setup\b/gi, '; Path=/');
  }

  if (isLocalhost(requestUrl)) {
    rewritten = rewritten.replace(/;\s*Secure/gi, '');
  }

  return rewritten;
}

function rewriteSetCookies(cookie: string, requestUrl: string): string[] {
  const rewritten = rewriteSetCookie(cookie, requestUrl);
  const shouldClearLegacyRefresh =
    /^hs_refresh=/i.test(cookie.trim()) &&
    /;\s*Path=\/api\/v1\/admin\/auth\/refresh/gi.test(cookie) &&
    /;\s*Max-Age=0\b/i.test(cookie);

  if (!shouldClearLegacyRefresh) return [rewritten];

  return [
    rewritten,
    rewriteSetCookie(
      cookie.replace(
        /;\s*Path=\/api\/v1\/admin\/auth\/refresh/gi,
        `; Path=${LEGACY_REFRESH_PROXY_PATH}`,
      ),
      requestUrl,
    ),
  ];
}

function buildSetupCookieClear(requestUrl: string): string {
  const secure = isLocalhost(requestUrl) ? '' : '; Secure';
  return `hs_setup=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; HttpOnly${secure}; SameSite=Strict`;
}

function buildForwardHeaders(request: Request): Headers {
  const headers = new Headers(request.headers);
  headers.delete('accept-encoding');
  headers.delete('connection');
  headers.delete('content-length');
  headers.delete('host');
  return headers;
}

function buildResponseHeaders(upstream: Response): Headers {
  const headers = new Headers();
  for (const [key, value] of upstream.headers.entries()) {
    const lower = key.toLowerCase();
    if (
      lower === 'content-encoding' ||
      lower === 'set-cookie' ||
      lower === 'transfer-encoding'
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    headers.set(key, value);
  }
  return headers;
}

async function proxy(request: Request, context: ProxyContext): Promise<NextResponse> {
  // CSRF protection via Origin allowlist.
  // SameSite=Strict on hs_access provides the primary CSRF defense.
  // This Origin check adds defense-in-depth for state-changing methods
  // without requiring cookie seeding (the double-submit cookie pattern
  // was half-implemented â€” see csrf.ts for the future full implementation).
  const unsafeMethod = !['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(request.method);
  if (unsafeMethod) {
    const origin = request.headers.get('origin');
    const allowed = process.env['NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL'] ?? 'http://localhost:3000';
    // Default-deny: missing Origin on unsafe methods is rejected. Browser fetches
    // always send Origin; server-to-server calls should not go through this proxy.
    if (origin !== allowed) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cross-origin request denied' }, { status: 403 });
    }
  }

  const { path = [] } = await context.params;
  const requestUrl = new URL(request.url);
  const apiPath = path.map((segment) => encodeURIComponent(segment)).join('/');
  const target = `${getApiBaseUrl()}/${apiPath}${requestUrl.search}`;
  const hasBody = request.method !== 'GET' && request.method !== 'HEAD';
  const init: RequestInit = {
    method: request.method,
    headers: buildForwardHeaders(request),
    redirect: 'manual',
    cache: 'no-store',
  };
  if (hasBody) {
    init.body = await request.arrayBuffer();
  }

  const upstream = await fetch(target, init);

  const response = new NextResponse(upstream.body, {
    status: upstream.status,
    statusText: upstream.statusText,
    headers: buildResponseHeaders(upstream),
  });

  for (const cookie of getSetCookieHeaders(upstream.headers)) {
    for (const rewritten of rewriteSetCookies(cookie, request.url)) {
      response.headers.append('set-cookie', rewritten);
    }
  }

  if (request.method === 'POST' && apiPath === SETUP_TOTP_PATH && upstream.ok) {
    response.headers.append('set-cookie', buildSetupCookieClear(request.url));
  }

  return response;
}

export const GET = proxy;
export const POST = proxy;
export const PUT = proxy;
export const PATCH = proxy;
export const DELETE = proxy;
export const OPTIONS = proxy;

The patch has functional gaps in the new PII reveal UI: technician phone controls are missing from the order list, and a prop change during an in-flight reveal can leave the reveal button permanently disabled. There is also a smaller error-handling issue for the new daily rate limit.

Full review comments:

- [P2] Show technician contact controls in the list — C:\Alok\Business Projects\wt-e09-s08\admin-web\src\components\orders\OrdersTable.tsx:69-69
  For assigned orders, the list view still renders only `TechnicianCell`, so `technicianPhoneMasked` is never shown or revealable from the table even though the story requires both customer and technician phone numbers to be masked by default in the list, drawer, and CSV (`docs/stories/E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:3-6`). Please render the technician `ContactReveal` here as well, or extend `TechnicianCell` to include it.

- [P2] Clear pending state when invalidating a reveal — C:\Alok\Business Projects\wt-e09-s08\admin-web\src\components\orders\ContactReveal.tsx:96-100
  When a reveal request is in flight and this component is reused for a different `orderId`, `party`, or `maskedPhone`, this effect invalidates the old request but leaves `pending` set to `true`; the stale request's `finally` branch then refuses to clear it because the request id no longer matches, so the new subject's "Show number" button stays disabled indefinitely. Reset `pending` in this subject-change effect along with the other reveal state.

- [P3] Preserve the daily rate-limit error — C:\Alok\Business Projects\wt-e09-s08\admin-web\src\components\orders\ContactReveal.tsx:24-28
  When the backend returns `429 RATE_LIMITED_DAILY` after the 50/day budget is exhausted, this status-only mapping collapses it into the per-minute `rateLimited` copy, telling the admin to try again in a minute even though the retry may be hours away. Preserve the response `code`/`retryAfterMs` in `RevealContactError` and add a distinct daily-cap message.

Full review comments:

- [P2] Show technician contact controls in the list — C:\Alok\Business Projects\wt-e09-s08\admin-web\src\components\orders\OrdersTable.tsx:69-69
  For assigned orders, the list view still renders only `TechnicianCell`, so `technicianPhoneMasked` is never shown or revealable from the table even though the story requires both customer and technician phone numbers to be masked by default in the list, drawer, and CSV (`docs/stories/E09-S08-pii-safe-phones.md:3-6`). Please render the technician `ContactReveal` here as well, or extend `TechnicianCell` to include it.

- [P2] Clear pending state when invalidating a reveal — C:\Alok\Business Projects\wt-e09-s08\admin-web\src\components\orders\ContactReveal.tsx:96-100
  When a reveal request is in flight and this component is reused for a different `orderId`, `party`, or `maskedPhone`, this effect invalidates the old request but leaves `pending` set to `true`; the stale request's `finally` branch then refuses to clear it because the request id no longer matches, so the new subject's "Show number" button stays disabled indefinitely. Reset `pending` in this subject-change effect along with the other reveal state.

- [P3] Preserve the daily rate-limit error — C:\Alok\Business Projects\wt-e09-s08\admin-web\src\components\orders\ContactReveal.tsx:24-28
  When the backend returns `429 RATE_LIMITED_DAILY` after the 50/day budget is exhausted, this status-only mapping collapses it into the per-minute `rateLimited` copy, telling the admin to try again in a minute even though the retry may be hours away. Preserve the response `code`/`retryAfterMs` in `RevealContactError` and add a distinct daily-cap message.
